| `pc-f4e26e858639` | length: description has maximum length 255 | `DATABASE_ENFORCED_UNHANDLED` | yes | database | no | unhandled | 0.95 | `zk-len-description-256`, `zk-update-len-description-256` | [zk_cluster_info.description: description has maximum length 255] An over-long description passed all application validation and reached the INSERT/UPDATE (MyBatis trace); H2 rejected it (JdbcSQLException 22001 'Value too long' / 22003 'Numeric value out of range') and the row was not persisted (read-back/404 or subsequent successful re-use). The console returns the raw MyBatis/JDBC exception text, including the SQL statement, to the client (GUI: HTTP 200 with status=1; REST: HTTP 500), i.e. the database rejection is not translated into a domain error. Create and (where applicable) update paths behave the same. |

## Warnings

- Input report is partial: 1 artifact failed extraction (pe-5ae5ef660866); targets from it are absent from scope.
- Only the H2 schema variant (dbctx-fe653de54ee4, 462 targets) was deployed; 925 MySQL/Docker-variant targets are VARIANT_NOT_DEPLOYED.
- Deployed H2 1.4.192 runs in MODE MySQL (db/h2/global.sql): NULL written to NOT NULL columns is coerced to ''/0 instead of rejected, so NOT NULL predicates are weaker at runtime than declared.
- The console ran inside a private unprivileged network namespace (loopback 127.0.0.1:18205/2181) because a stale console from an earlier, unrelated run still occupies those host-loopback ports; that process was not touched.
- raw/run1-no-sqltrace holds an identical first run executed before SQL DEBUG tracing was enabled; results cite the second, traced run.
